## Critical Rules (Never Forget)

- **`'use client'` boundary** — Only add when you need browser APIs, hooks, or interactivity. Keep everything else server-side.
- **Server Components can't use hooks** — No `useState`, `useEffect`, `useContext` in server components.
- **Colocate data fetching** — Fetch data in the component that needs it, not prop-drill from the top.
- **Zod for all schema validation** — Never trust raw form/API data without validation.
- **Tailwind = utility-first** — Don't fight it. Compose utilities, don't fight the cascade.
- **Server Actions = mutations** — Use them for form submits and data mutations; fetch for reads.
- **`<Image>` over `<img>`** — Always use Next's Image for automatic optimization and CLS prevention.
- **Abort in-flight requests** — Use `AbortController` to prevent race conditions on navigation.
- **Types in, types out** — Mirror backend response types exactly. No `any` from API calls.

The new `parentEnter` / `parentExit` props (on `<ViewTransition>`) take `className` / `name` arrays and apply a view transition at the *parent container* level when any of its children transition. Gated behind the `enableViewTransitionParentEnterExit` feature flag in React; set in `react/src/ReactFeatureFlags.js` (default off). Use case: animate the page chrome (header, side nav) when a child component cross-fades, so the chrome transitions with the child rather than staying still underneath. Full details in patterns.md → ViewTransition parentEnter/parentExit. New `experimental.durableUseCacheEntries?: boolean` flag in `experimentalSchema` (`packages/next/src/server/config-schema.ts`) plus its `ExperimentalConfig` declaration (`config-shared.ts`). **Turbopack-only.** Surface shows up as a new `codeHash` field in `.next/server/server-reference-manifest.json`. Disabled in dev. Known limitation: `final_read_hint` makes some chunk items codegen twice. Multiple `use cache` functions in a single file share the same hash. Full details in performance.md → durable use cache code hash; **(I) [PR #95281](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95281) — `Fix workers on thread to not crash with napi-rs`** by jimmyhmiller, merged 2026-06-30T19:38:35Z — Turbopack worker-on-thread crashes traced to a [napi-rs bug](https://github.com/napi-rs/napi-rs/issues/3357) about dropping things off the isolate; workaround now in place so worker threads don't crash. Default still set to process workers (so impact is minimal until user opts into thread workers); **(J) [PR #95282](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95282) — `Upgrade React from 68631c04-20260626 to 92f4fda3-20260629`** by Vercel Release Bot, merged 2026-06-30T20:10:46Z — vendored React bump inside `packages/next/src/compiled/react/cjs/react.production.js`. Brings [React PR #36851](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36851) `[Float] Forward nonce option in ReactDOM.preloadModule()` (the silent CSP violation fix from the 12:38 UTC cron) into canary.72's bundled React so it's used for SSR/RSC even if your `package.json` pins React to an older canary. The canary.72 release-time React version is `19.3.0-canary-92f4fda3-20260629` (verified by reading `exports.version` from the bundled file); **(K) [PR #95204](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95204) — `[ci] Add support for auth based preview build registries`** by eps1lon, merged 2026-06-30T18:59:16Z — CI-only follow-up to [#93464](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/93464); no user-facing impact.. **Full canary.71 PR-by-PR breakdown in the existing canary.71 paragraph below this opening line**; **16.3.0-canary.65** released June 24, 2026 (Cache Components docs clarify `allow-runtime` / sync-IO / `instant = false` / CLS fallback ([#94997](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94997)), `cacheMaxMemorySize: 0` no longer forces a dynamic cache life in dev so a fully cached route can prerender again ([#95100](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95100)), `getHeaders` no longer mutates `req.headers` when stripping internal headers — fixes a dev instant-navigation regression where the React dev overlay stayed in "Rendering..." after a server action redirect because the wrong HTML request id was fabricated from a missing request id ([#95116](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95116))); **16.3.0-canary.67** released June 24–25, 2026 (Restore canary version 16.3.0-canary.66 after v16.3.0-preview.4 preview release, **fix local fonts in statically prerendered `ImageResponse` metadata route** ([#95121](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95121) — Node `Buffer.toJSON` round-trip corruption, follow-up to #94957; cache key is now SHA-256 of serialized element + content hash of options, font bytes hashed by their bytes, options paired in-memory to avoid Flight round-trip), **docs(`root-params`): `generateStaticParams` section and CC requirement** ([#95073](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95073) — with `cacheComponents: true`, every root param must have a value in `generateStaticParams` or the build fails), **surface an error for blocking routes under the Navigation Inspector** ([#95139](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95139) — empty static shell detection, clears the instant-navigation cookie, no more "blank document, no DevTools, can't reload out" footgun), **suppress `prefetch={true}` warning when route opts out via `instant = false`** ([#95099](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95099) — new `SubtreeHasInstantFalse` `PrefetchHint` propagated to root, dev warning skips routes with this bit, warning-only and separate from `PrefetchDisabled`), **`next-cache-components-adoption` skill update** ([#95122](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95122) — leads with `next-dev-loop` recommendation, adds build-only verification path for CI/sandboxed agents, hoists skill pointer to top-level `## Use the adoption skill (recommended)` H2 in `migrating-to-cache-components.mdx`)); Need to figure out what config it is actually reading from."; the `experimental.ppr` config + `isAppPPREnabled` + `__NEXT_PPR` are BACK, error code #1375 is GONE, `remove-experimental-ppr` codemod is reverted; the skill's "use `cacheComponents: true` for all PPR behavior" guidance still holds), `<Link prefetch={true}>` Partial Prefetching warning wired into Instant Insights via shared `instant-messages.ts` factory ([#94798](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94798) — code frame + call stack + three fix cards: Upgrade / Disable / Ignore), Preview tarballs available while new canaries publish ([#95112](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95112)), ad-hoc preview release cut from canary ([#95086](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95086) — preview releases now cut from canary with bump-to-preview + revert-to-canary commits, base = max(canary, @preview) version), Fix Navigation Inspector styles in dark mode ([#95126](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95126))); opt-in to automatic native binding install in CI is preserved for `test_examples` only); **16.3.0-preview.5** released June 25, 2026 ([`70543c5`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.3.0-preview.5) — `instant()` only renders the static shell unless `{ prefetch: true }` is passed ([#95150](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95150) — `<Link prefetch>` is unaffected, custom integration code that expected the full payload must now pass `prefetch: true`), dev now replicates production shell-only prefetch behavior ([#95067](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95067) — closes a long-standing dev/prod discrepancy that masked shell-only correctness bugs until ship-time), `next-dev-loop` papercut fixes ([#95153](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95153)), docs: expand `io()` reference ([#95147](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95147))); **16.3.0-canary.71** released June 29, 2026 ([`dff50cc736`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/commit/dff50cc736) — 17 canary-branch commits since canary.70, total diff at [`compare/v16.3.0-canary.70...v16.3.0-canary.71`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/compare/v16.3.0-canary.70...v16.3.0-canary.71), tag cut at 2026-06-29T23:36:11Z, npm `dist-tag.next` pointer moved 2026-06-29T23:59:44Z). The 17 commits since canary.70 split into **eight material user-facing changes** (six Next.js canary-branch PRs + one Turbopack PR + one React canary PR that became the new canary-`92f4fda3-20260629`): **(1) fix: preserve repeated search params in client page segment cache keys** ([#94863](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/94863) by icyJoseph, merged June 29, 2026 at 11:54:29Z — silent stale-UI bug where `Object.fromEntries(new URLSearchParams(search))` collapsed `?foo=bar&foo=baz` to `{foo: ‘baz’}` in the client page segment cache key, causing cache collisions between `?color=red&color=blue` and `?color=blue`; affected `<Link>`, `router.replace`, `<form>` (next/form) soft-nav with repeated keys; fixed in `ppr-navigations.ts` + `segment-cache/scheduler.ts` + `route-params.ts` by switching to the existing `urlSearchParamsToParsedUrlQuery` helper which preserves repeated params as arrays; fixes issues [#92787](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/92787) (filed against next 16.2.1-canary.38, April 14), [#93104](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/93104) (filed against stable, April 21), and [#94821](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/94821) (filed against canary.51, June 15 — next/form submission case); new e2e test at `test/e2e/app-dir/multi-value-search-params-stale/`; no API change, no config change, no opt-in flag; single-value URLs unaffected; **upgrade to canary.71+ or 16.3.0 stable to get the fix**; full details in routing.md); **(2) fix: client nav URL bar stuck on original URL after middleware redirect + rewrite-to-dynamic-route** ([#95207](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/95207) by acdlite / Andrew Clark, merged June 29, 2026 at 17:00:28Z — silent stale-URL bug where a click on `<Link href="/a">` with a proxy/middleware redirect `/a → /` AND rewrite `/ → /a` (where `/a` is a dynamic route reading `await params`) rendered the correct page content but left `window.location.href` stuck on `/a` instead of `/`; only fires on soft-nav, hard-nav was always correct via the server resolver; root cause was the optimistic-URL-commit in the Instant Navigations reducer — the prediction was committed up front and the resolved RSC payload (after the redirect) was treated as confirmation of the prediction when the redirect target had the same shape as the prediction; fix invalidates the prediction on a redirect discovered during navigation and re-resolves the route before the address bar is shown; touches `ppr-navigations.ts` + `reducers/server-patch-reducer.ts` + `router-reducer-types.ts`; new e2e test at `test/e2e/app-dir/redirect-rewrite-dynamic/`; **fixes [issue #95195](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/95195) by amannn against 16.3.0-preview.5** with a [public Playwright reproduction repo](https://github.com/amannn/nextjs-16-3-bug-repro-redirect); full details in routing.md); **(3) Turbopack `experimental.cssChunking: ‘graph’` config schema breaking change** ([#95088](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95088) by sokra / Tobias Koppers, merged June 29, 2026 at 15:53:24Z — `moduleFactorCost` option REMOVED, replaced by `weightDistribution`; new per-group cost formula `chunk_group_weight * (chunk_size + request_cost)` where `chunk_group_weight = group_total_size ^ (-weightDistribution)`; defaults retuned: `requestCost` `20000` → `100000` bytes, `weightDistribution` default `0.1`; old `{ type: ‘graph’, requestCost, moduleFactorCost }` config throws a schema error in canary.71+ — string form `‘graph’` is unchanged and uses the new defaults automatically; full details in performance.md). **Plus [React canary `19.3.0-canary-92f4fda3-20260629`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react) (cut 2026-06-29T17:11:25Z, replaces canary `68631c04-20260626`)** — 3 commits ahead of the previous canary, one of which is material. *(As of 2026-06-30T16:54:01Z, the React canary SHA has moved forward to `19.3.0-canary-e2731312-20260630` (replaces `92f4fda3-20260629`). The new canary is non-material for the skill — its 6 commits are devtools-facade work and a chrome-devtools-mcp integration; no new APIs or fixes relevant to documented patterns.)* **[React PR #36851](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36851) `[Float] Forward nonce option in ReactDOM.preloadModule()`** by udit / Udit Dewan, merged June 29, 2026 at 11:33:22Z — silent CSP violation where `ReactDOM.preloadModule(href, { nonce, as, ... })` accepted `nonce` in its public type but the implementation only forwarded `as`, `crossOrigin`, and `integrity` to the host dispatcher, dropping `nonce`; under strict `script-src ‘nonce-...’` CSP, the emitted `<link rel="modulepreload">` had no nonce and was blocked by the browser; `ReactDOM.preload()` and `ReactDOM.preinitModule()` already forwarded `nonce` correctly; 5 files, +37/-5 across `ReactDOMFloat.js`, `ReactDOMTypes.js`, `ReactFlightClientConfigDOM.js`, and the test suite; full details in security.md). The other 6 canary-branch commits since canary.70 are non-material (skill docs, CI, React upgrade bump, type improvement, internal Turbopack): `cc-adoption` skill tighten by Aurora Scharff ([#95225](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95225), docs-only), `unstable_rethrow` client-bundling fix by eps1lon ([#95196](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95196), internal bundling optimization, Closes #95189), Turbopack `server-reference-manifest` dedup by Tariqul ([#95242](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95242), internal bug fix), CC test command display fix by eps1lon ([#95267](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95267), CI), React canary bump in Next.js by Vercel Release Bot ([#95211](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95211), package.json only), `decodeReply` typing by mischnic ([#95263](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95263), type narrowing), `[changelog] Add 19.0.x, 19.1.x, 19.2.x patch releases` by eps1lon ([#36898](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36898), changelog only), `[test] coverage for multi-byte code units in stream APIs` by eps1lon ([#36900](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36900), test only); **16.3.0-canary.70** released June 28, 2026 (npm dist-tag pointer moved 2026-06-29T00:02:51Z) — only one new content commit ([#95232](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95232) by mischnic, `Turbopack: make hash_xxh3_hash128 return u128` — pure Rust internal type-narrowing refactor, zero user-facing impact, all files under `turbopack/crates/`); **16.3.0-canary.69** released June 26, 2026 ([`4b164d7`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.3.0-canary.69), 15 commits since canary.68) — **fix: preserve middleware rewrite query in Pages API routes** ([#94905](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/94905), fixes issue [#94647](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/94647), June 10 — middleware rewrites from `/foo/bar?key=value` to `/api/proxy/bar?added=1&extra=2` were silently dropping the rewrite-added `added`/`extra` query params AND the destination catch-all `slug` param from `req.query` in Pages API handlers; regression from Next.js 14.2.35 introduced during the Pages API handler-interface migration, affected both webpack and Turbopack; `NextNodeServer.runApi` now forwards resolved `query` and `match.params` through the bundled handler context while preserving the original `req.url`; full details in routing.md), **hard-navigate to app routes shadowed by a pages dynamic route** ([#95185](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/95185), shipped in canary.69 — soft-nav misroute fix, full details in routing.md), dev overlay Copy-as-prompt body restructured into step-by-step checklist ([#95186](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95186)), docs: insights error pages restructure + link to preview docs ([#95193](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95193)), docs: reorder blocking-prerender-dynamic fixes to Stream/Cache/Block ([#95198](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95198)), `next-dev-loop` requires `agent-browser >= 0.31.1` ([#95209](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95209)), Turbopack Rust toolchain bump ([#95205](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95205)), docs: fix preview message typo ([#95050](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95050)), Turbopack internal: revert `final_read_hint` ([#95178](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95178)) — first canary cut after the preview.5 stabilisation, so #94905 + #95185 are the two user-facing changes; everything else is docs/dev-overlay/agent-skill/tooling/internal metrics)
- **React 19.2.8** stable (shipped 2026-07-21T15:49:09Z, [GitHub release](https://github.com/facebook/react/releases/tag/v19.2.8); 19.2.7 → 19.2.8 diff is PR #37087 `[FlightReply] Performance improvements when decoding` + PR #36753 housekeeping) + **React 19.2.x canary** [`81e442ea-20260721`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react?activeTab=canary) (ships Fragment-blur fix + nested ViewTransition) — React Compiler 1.0 stable, `use()` hook, `useOptimistic`, `useFormStatus`, `useActionState`, `useEffectEvent`, `cacheSignal`, `cache`, `<Activity>`
- **TypeScript 6.0.3** (strict by default, ES2026 target, import defer) + **TypeScript 7.0.1-rc** (June 18, 2026, RC release of Go-based compiler ships as the main `typescript` package — `npx tsc` is now Go, ~10× faster, Strada internal API; stable targeted within 2 months of RC)
- **Zod 4.4.3** (14x faster string parsing, strict/loose object modes, `z.file()`, `z.templateLiteral()`)
- **Tailwind CSS v4.3.2** + **shadcn/ui 4.13.0** (CSS-first config via `@theme` directive — no tailwind.config.js by default; **shadcn 4.13.0 (July 3, 2026)** flips the default headless lib to **Base UI** (Radix still supported as the explicit alternative; per [official changelog](https://ui.shadcn.com/docs/changelog/2026-07-base-ui-default) this is the result of wide community adoption); **shadcn/typeset (July 14, 2026)** — new stream-friendly typography system (single CSS file, three CSS variables per container, no package, no config layer, container-aware, pairs naturally with shadcn 4.12.0 Chat Components for streaming markdown in chat UIs); **v4.3.2 (June 29, 2026) pure bug-fix patch** — `auto-rows-*` / `auto-cols-*` accept bare numeric values (#20229), `@tailwindcss/cli --watch` no longer crashes on Windows when `@source` points to a missing directory (#20242), `@tailwindcss/vite` no longer crashes in Deno v2.8.x (#20245), `@tailwindcss/cli --watch` no longer re-emits an unchanged file when only a sibling changes (#20249); v4.3.1 patch added `--silent` CLI flag, `@apply` with CSS mixins, cleaner spacing output; **4.11.1 (June 26, 2026) patches `shadcn add` to preserve existing `package.json` dependency specifiers** — fixes [shadcn-ui/ui#10525](https://github.com/shadcn-ui/ui/issues/10525) where the CLI could swap specifier values between packages, leaving lockfile + manifest contradictory; also drops the `node-fetch` transitive dep in favor of native `fetch` ([#10905](https://github.com/shadcn-ui/ui/pull/10905)); **4.12.0 (June 26, 2026, same day as 4.11.1) ships the first phase of Chat Components** — `MessageScroller` / `Message` / `Bubble` / `Attachment` / `Marker` (each available in Base UI + Radix UI flavors), two new CSS utilities `scroll-fade` + `shimmer` (shipped via `shadcn/tailwind.css`, so any project initialized with `npx shadcn@latest init` gets them for free), and a brand-new npm package **`@shadcn/react@0.1.0`** — unstyled headless React primitives, first primitive is `@shadcn/react/message-scroller` with the same interaction logic the registry item wraps)
- **Modern CSS 2026** — `field-sizing: content` (Baseline 2026, Firefox 152 just shipped), `@starting-style` (Baseline 2024), CSS Anchor Positioning (cross-engine 2025–26), OKLCH color space, `color-scheme` + `light-dark()` function, scroll-driven animations
- **Vite 8.1.0** (for non-Next projects; Next uses Turbopack — 8.1 ships WASM ESM Integration, `server.hmr` → `server.ws` rename, `import.meta.glob` `caseSensitive`, chunk importmap, lightningcss support, extended `server.fs.deny` defaults; **≥ 8.1.0 required for the new `caseSensitive` glob option**)
- **Vite 8.1.2** (June 30, 2026 patch — pure bug-fix release that supersedes 8.1.1; **8.1.2 is the recommended Vite version** because it: (1) reverts the es-module-lexer 2.2.0 upgrade in 8.1.1 (PR #22827) which broke any prod output chunk >~4 MB with `WebAssembly.Memory.grow()` error ([guybedford/es-module-lexer#214](https://github.com/guybedford/es-module-lexer/issues/214), reported as Vite [#22826](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/issues/22826)); (2) reverts PR #22687 multi-null-byte id escape from 8.1.1 because it broke the **waku** and **vite-plugin-rsc** ecosystem-ci suites; (3) re-applies the 8.1.1 pnpm `.modules.yaml` workspace-root resolution (PR #22825, with a corrected implementation of the originally-intended #22757); and (4) retains all the 8.1.1-intended fixes (`resolve.tsconfigPaths` CSS/Sass regression fix #22775, Bundled Dev Mode `import.meta.hot.invalidate()` stack overflow #22797, Bundled Dev Mode HMR/lazy-compile asset serving #22745, `esbuild.jsxSideEffects` → `oxc.jsx.pure` inversion #22809, `indexHtmlMiddleware` malformed URI crash #22781, missing sourcemap field in plugins #22782, `ERR_CLOSED_SERVER` in optimize-deps scanner #22784). **Skip 8.1.1 entirely** — go 8.1.0 → 8.1.2. See setup.md → Vite 8.1.2 for the long-form patterns, the `tsconfig.json` `include` requirement for CSS/Sass files, and the 8.1.1/8.1.2/es-module-lexer migration matrix)
- **@biomejs/biome 2.5.2** (recommended linter/formatter — 10–100x faster than ESLint, v2 has breaking changes from v1; run `npx biome migrate --write` after every upgrade)
- **TanStack Query v5.101.2** (React Query v5 — gcTime replaces cacheTime, improved SSR hydration, `skipToken` for dependent queries; **5.101.2 (June 27, 2026)** patches `@tanstack/query-devtools` — `setupStyleSheet` now sets `window.__nonce__` when `styleNonce` is passed ([#10736](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10736), fixes a silent CSP violation where the devtools' `<style>` tag was injected without a nonce and tripped strict `style-src 'nonce-...'` policies in production), `PiPContext.closePipWindow` now resets `pip_open` in localStorage to stop auto-reopen ([#10813](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10813)), `setupStyleSheet` dedup scoped to shadow-DOM target ([#10815](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10815)), `last-updated` sort returns 0 on ties so the comparator is deterministic ([#10812](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10812)), Theme sub-trigger className typo `position` → `theme` ([#10811](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10811)))
- **Vitest 4.1.9** (Browser Mode stable, Visual Regression testing via `toMatchScreenshot`, Playwright Trace support; requires Vite ≥ 6 + Node.js ≥ 20; **≥ 4.1.8 required for the CDP RCE fix (GHSA-g8mr-85jm-7xhm, CVSS 9.8)**; use `api.allowWrite: false, api.allowExec: false` in CI; **Vitest 5.0 in beta** — three beta releases between May 19 and June 15, 2026 (beta.3, beta.4, beta.5) introduced hard requirement Node 22 + Vite 6.4, strict `toHaveTextContent` + new `toMatchTextContent`, Browser Mode `locators.exact: true` default, `expect.poll` fails on timeout, hoistable-methods-out-of-scope throw, removed entry points (`vitest/coverage` → `vitest/node`, `vitest/environments` / `vitest/snapshot` → `vitest/runtime`, `vitest/runners` / `vitest/suite` → `TestRunner` from `vitest`, `vitest/mocker` → `@vitest/mocker`), `bench` moved into `test()` fixture, `test.sequential` → `{ concurrent: false }`, no ancestor-directory config lookup, `@vitest/runner` inlined into `vitest`, `TestModule.id` 1-based, `coverage.thresholds.perFile` accepts an object, browser orchestrator requires `sessionId`)
- **@playwright/test 1.61.1** (E2E — **1.61.0 (June 15, 2026)** shipped WebAuthn passkeys virtual authenticator via `browserContext.credentials.create/install`, `page.localStorage` / `page.sessionStorage` (origin-aware, replaces `page.evaluate('localStorage…')` boilerplate), `apiResponse.securityDetails()` / `serverAddr()` network parity with browser responses, `testOptions.video` modes parity with trace (`'on-all-retries'`, `'retain-on-first-failure'`, `'retain-on-failure-and-retries'`), `expect.soft.poll(...)`, `fullConfig.argv`, `fullConfig.failOnFlakyTests`, `testInfo.errors` AggregateError sub-entries, `-G` shorthand for `--grep-invert`, HAR + trace now include WebSocket frames, Ubuntu 26.04 host support, browsers Chromium 149.0.7827.55 / Firefox 151.0 / WebKit 26.5; **1.61.1 (June 23, 2026)** fixed five 1.61 regressions — `expect.extend` matcher-name shadowing built-ins, UI-mode API request byte count mismatch, trace-viewer WebSocket timestamps scaled by 1/1000, sync ESM loader crash on Node 22.15, pnpm workspace symlink `.ts` subpath resolution)
- **React Hook Form v7.80.0** + **@hookform/resolvers v5.4.0** (7.80.0 ships per-field `disabled` on `useFieldArray` items; broad perf pass; fixes `[]` vs `{}` `deepEqual` regression from 7.79.0) (compatible with Zod v4; v8.0.0-beta available with `createForm` API)
- **Node.js 24 LTS** (Node.js 22 LTS also supported)

## Pro Tips

- Use `create-next-app --typescript --tailwind --eslint --app --src-dir --import-alias "@/*"` for a fully typed Next.js project
- Run `npx tsc --noEmit` to type-check without building
- Use `shadcn@latest add <component>` to add components — they live in your codebase, not node_modules
- Use `next.config.ts` for all Next.js configuration (not `.js`)
- Use `react-hook-form` + `zod` for all forms — never manage form state with useState
- Enable React Compiler in `next.config.ts` with `reactCompiler: true` — eliminates most `useMemo`/`useCallback` manually
- Use Biome (`npx biome check`) for linting — 10–100x faster than ESLint
- Run `npx biome migrate --write` after every Biome upgrade to migrate your config for breaking changes
- Use `use cache` for all server-side data fetching in Next.js 16 — it's the explicit opt-in caching model

Start with `setup.md` to initialize a project, then `components.md` for shadcn/ui patterns.

## Next.js 16 Migration Notes

Key breaking changes from Next.js 15 → 16:

| Change | Impact |
|---|---|
| **`use cache` directive** replaces `unstable_cache` | New explicit caching API — mark data functions with `use cache` + `cacheTag` |
| **Implicit caching removed** — everything is dynamic by default | Old `fetch` caching patterns deprecated; use `use cache` explicitly |
| **PPR (Partial Prerendering) stable** — `cacheComponents: true` | Static shell + streaming dynamic content; no longer experimental |
| **Router scroll optimization** enabled by default | Previously scroll was reset on navigation; now preserved by default |
| Flat config default in `@next/eslint-plugin-next` | ESLint config format change |
| Deprecated `.turbo` config object removed | Use `turbopack` key in `next.config.ts` instead |
| `publicRuntimeConfig` / `serverRuntimeConfig` removed | Use environment variables directly |
| **`next lint` removed** | Use Biome (`npx biome check`) or ESLint (`npx eslint .`) directly |
| **`middleware.ts` deprecated → `proxy.ts`** | `middleware` export renamed to `proxy`; must be `async`; `matcher` moved to `next.config.ts` or exported from `proxy.ts` |

**Upgrade:** `npx @next/codemod@canary upgrade latest` (Next.js 16.2+) — automated upgrade CLI that handles codemods, config migrations, and breaking changes in one command. **In canary.72+ (PR #95312, June 30, 2026) accepts `-y, --yes` for agents/CI** — auto-enabled when stdin is not a TTY so AI coding agents no longer hang on the recommended-codemods multiselect. Alternatively `npx next upgrade` (interactive, Next.js 16.1+) or `npm install next@latest`.

## 1.4.77 — 2026-07-21T18:03Z

**JULY 2026 SECURITY RELEASE — ALL 9 CVEs NOW DOCUMENTED.** The July 21, 2026 security release shipped at ~16:00 UTC (16.2.11 / 15.5.21). Full CVE table added to `security.md`: 4 High (DoS via Server Actions, Turbopack middleware bypass with single i18n locale, SSRF via rewrites, SSRF via Server Actions on custom servers) + 5 Medium (Image Optimization SVG DoS, unbounded Edge payload, Server Function endpoint disclosure, cache confusion ×2). The live status checkpoints (T-24h through T+6h) removed and replaced with final resolution. `api.md` gained "Server Action Security Hardening (canary.92)" section covering 7 PRs: bodySizeLimit Edge enforcement, correct origin for custom-server redirects, exotic rewrite param encoding, server reference ID validation (closes CVE-2026-64643), basePath preservation in redirects, and two fetch cache key fixes (effective-request keying + byte-exact binary body hashing). `deployment.md` gained "July 2026 Security Releases" section documenting 16.2.11 (Active LTS) + 15.5.21 (Maintenance LTS) + canary.92 upgrade path, post-upgrade checklist, and the monthly cadence reminder (next: August 20, 2026). canary.92 non-security items: Turbopack middleware i18n single-locale fix, MPA form validation perf, SVG detectContentType perf, instant validation navigation fix, `use cache` error handling, docs AI agents guide expansion. React 19.2.8 (2026-07-21) — performance improvements when decoding RSC (PR #37087, eps1lon). TanStack Query 5.101.4 (2026-07-21) — patch on query-core 5.101.4.

Sources:
- [Next.js blog: July 2026 Security Release](https://nextjs.org/blog/july-2026-security-release)
- [GitHub: v16.2.11](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.2.11) · [v15.5.21](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v15.5.21) · [v16.3.0-canary.92](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.3.0-canary.92)
- [React v19.2.8](https://github.com/facebook/react/releases/tag/v19.2.8) · [TanStack Query 5.101.4](https://github.com/TanStack/query/releases/tag/release-2026-07-21-1305)

## Critical Rules (Never Forget)

- **`'use client'` boundary** — Only add when you need browser APIs, hooks, or interactivity. Keep everything else server-side.
- **Server Components can't use hooks** — No `useState`, `useEffect`, `useContext` in server components.
- **Colocate data fetching** — Fetch data in the component that needs it, not prop-drill from the top.
- **Zod for all schema validation** — Never trust raw form/API data without validation.
- **Tailwind = utility-first** — Don't fight it. Compose utilities, don't fight the cascade.
- **Server Actions = mutations** — Use them for form submits and data mutations; fetch for reads.
- **`<Image>` over `<img>`** — Always use Next's Image for automatic optimization and CLS prevention.
- **Abort in-flight requests** — Use `AbortController` to prevent race conditions on navigation.
- **Types in, types out** — Mirror backend response types exactly. No `any` from API calls.

New `DynamicHoleKind.Link = 1` in `packages/next/src/server/app-render/dynamic-rendering.ts` (renumbers `Runtime` 1→2 and `Dynamic` 2→3); new stage `ShellRuntime` added between `Static` and `Runtime` in `instant-validation.tsx`'s `SEGMENT_STAGE_ORDER`; new error builders `createLinkBodyErrorInNavigation` (error 1390), `createLinkMetadataError` (error 1391), `createLinkViewportError` (error 1392) in `packages/next/src/server/app-render/blocking-route-messages.ts`; new entries in `packages/next/errors.json`. Detection: if a hole is present in `ShellRuntime` but disappears in `Runtime`, it's link data (link data can resolve in Runtime but not in App Shell). 21 new test fixtures in `test/e2e/app-dir/instant-validation/app/shells/(default)/` (8 valid + 6 invalid route combos × instant config matrix); plus new `valid-session-only` / `valid-session-with-dynamic` fixtures prove session data is allowed outside Suspense in App Shells. **Known limitation (author-noted):** uses chunks from the dev render that resolve static params in Static stage, so static params are incorrectly included in ShellRuntime and don't trigger link-data errors — fixed by follow-up PR #94595. **(M) [PR #94595](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94595) — `[PP] Instant validation - error for unguarded static params`** by Janka Uryga, merged 2026-07-01T06:59:43Z — follow-up to #95151 extending link-data detection to `generateStaticParams`. Static params resolve in `Static` (for HTML-shell validation, initial-load path) vs `ShellRuntime` (for client-nav on `partialPrefetching: true`) via the new `requestStore.needsSessionShell` flag (DEV-only, set in three places in `app-render.tsx`). Three params utilities moved from inline definitions in `params.ts` into new `packages/next/src/server/lib/params-utils.ts` (`isEmptyParams`, `hasFallbackRouteParams`, `allParamsAreRootParams` + new `hasNonRootStaticParams`); `app-render.tsx` calls `hasNonRootStaticParams` to decide whether to skip the secondary render (optimization when no static params exist). **Known limitation (author-noted):** pre-existing `fallbackParams` bug in build validation (two tests marked `// TODO(app-shells): missing fallback params in build validation`); also doesn't touch `environmentName` (deliberately, follow-up PR coming). **(N) [#95336](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95336)** revert of test runner migration** — CI only, no user-facing impact. **(O) [#95106](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95106)** refactor: split out validateStaticShell** — internal refactor. **(P) [#95332](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95332)** `[ci] Move some test suites back to x86-64 runners`** — reverted by #95336, net no-op. When the codemod bumps the installed `eslint-config-next` to the target version, it now also bumps `eslint` to a version that satisfies the new `eslint-config-next`'s peer dependency (reads the package's `peerDependencies.eslint` range and resolves the highest matching `eslint@latest` from the registry). Without this, default CNA-15 projects hit `ERESOLVE` from `eslint-config-next`'s new peer dep and the codemod loop fails. Tested against the `cna-15.0.0-app` fixture; same agent-loop motivation as #95312. The new `parentEnter` / `parentExit` props (on `<ViewTransition>`) take `className` / `name` arrays and apply a view transition at the *parent container* level when any of its children transition. Gated behind the `enableViewTransitionParentEnterExit` feature flag in React; set in `react/src/ReactFeatureFlags.js` (default off). Use case: animate the page chrome (header, side nav) when a child component cross-fades, so the chrome transitions with the child rather than staying still underneath. Full details in patterns.md → ViewTransition parentEnter/parentExit. full details in deployment.md), **docs(`turbopackRustReactCompiler`)** ([#95280](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95280) canary.71 — 52-line `turbopackRustReactCompiler.mdx` docs page for the existing `experimental.rustReactCompiler` flag from canary.52 and the recently-added `experimental.turbopackRustReactCompiler` flag). **Plus [React canary `19.3.0-canary-92f4fda3-20260629`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react)** (cut 2026-06-29T17:11:25Z, replaces canary `68631c04-20260626`) — one material change: **[React PR #36851](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36851) `[Float] Forward nonce option in ReactDOM.preloadModule()`** (silent CSP violation where the public type accepted `nonce` but the implementation only forwarded `as`/`crossOrigin`/`integrity`; under strict `script-src 'nonce-...'` CSP, the emitted `<link rel="modulepreload">` had no nonce and was blocked; `ReactDOM.preload()`/`ReactDOM.preinitModule()` already forwarded `nonce` correctly; 5 files, +37/-5; full details in security.md). **canary.72 is now the latest canary** (22 commits vs canary.71, see [compare/v16.3.0-canary.71...v16.3.0-canary.72](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/compare/v16.3.0-canary.71...v16.3.0-canary.72)). The 22 commits split into **two distinct groups**: **(A) The 17 in-flight commits already documented at the previous cron (18:14 UTC)** — all of which are now officially shipped in canary.72 — six were material: **(A) [PR #95289](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95289) — `fix: params/searchParams in client page crashing dev instant validation`** by Janka Uryga, merged 2026-06-30T14:38:02Z — silent dev crash with `Invariant: Expected to have a workUnitStore that provides validationSampleTracking. This is a bug in Next.js.` redbox whenever a `'use client'` component reads `await props.params` or `await props.searchParams` via `use()` on a Cache Components (`cacheComponents: true`) dynamic route; dev-only (build passes, CI suites miss it); caused by the `'validation-client'` case in `createParamsFromClient` (`packages/next/src/server/request/params.ts`) and `createSearchParamsFromClient` (`search-params.ts`) wrapping params in a validation-sample-tracking proxy that needs `workUnitStore.validationSamples` (not set in dev-instant-validation); fix removes the case from params and gates it on `workUnitStore.validationSamples` for search-params; 9 new e2e tests in `test/e2e/app-dir/instant-validation/`; full details in routing.md; **(B) [PR #95216](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95216) — `Log content-free diagnostics when a postponed state fails to parse`** by Hendrik Liebau, merged 2026-06-30T08:58:13Z — `parsePostponedState` was logging only the bare underlying error which gave operators no way to distinguish a truncated body, a corrupt resume-data cache, or a body that wasn't a postponed state at all; the invariant thrown for a body without a length prefix also embedded the entire raw state in its message (can be large, may contain sensitive serialized data) — fix emits a content-free diagnostic tag instead; **(C) [PR #95269](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95269) — `Surface empty generateStaticParams as a redbox with a real stack`** by Hendrik Liebau, merged 2026-06-30T11:18:53Z — SWC transform emits `__next_create_empty_gsp_error` factory span-mapped back to source; gated on `cacheComponents: true`; known limitation: wildcard re-exports (`export * from`) not covered; **(D) [PR #95270](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95270) — `Render a code frame for build errors thrown collecting page data`** by Hendrik Liebau, merged 2026-06-30T11:18:54Z — installs native bindings + `installCodeFrameSupport` in static worker entry (only in worker entry, not `build/utils.ts` to avoid pulling devtools into `next-server-nft`); only renders when source map carries source content (`serverSourceMaps: true` / `--debug-prerender`); **(E) [PR #95286](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95286) — `Turbopack SWC: constant-evaluate 'x in y' when x is literal`** by Niklas Mischkulnig, merged 2026-06-30T11:04:56Z — new `JsValue::In` / `Effect::In` / `JsValue::r#in` in turbopack-ecmascript analyzer; **(F) [PR #94811](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94811) — `Log config-evaluation time`** by Luke Sandberg, merged 2026-06-30T06:36:48Z — `✓ next.config.ts evaluated in Xms` line in dev startup and `next build` startup; use to diagnose slow config. The remaining 11 in-flight commits are non-material: `95211` React canary bump (package.json only), `95217` relay-compiler 21.0.1 CI bump, `95283` build-log code-frame width 100→240 columns, `95288` `bool→enum Modified` Rust internal refactor, `95297` / `95303` / `95304` / `95307` / `95308` test infrastructure (Turbopack NFT unskip, webpack server-action-period-hash timeout, leftover build process retry on test retry, .next deletion ENOTEMPTY retry, build-output-prerender error ordering), `95148` docs (remove unnecessary async from rewrites/headers/redirects examples), `94870` arm64 CI runner migration. **These in-flight material PRs are reflected in the SKILL topic table as `shipped in canary.72`.** **(B) FIVE NEW commits landed on the canary branch after the previous cron (18:14 UTC June 30), all merged into canary.72:** **(G) [PR #95312](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95312) — `codemod: non-interactive upgrade for agents and CI`** by aurorascharff, merged 2026-06-30T20:03:37Z — **most impactful for AI-agent workflows this cycle**. `pnpm dlx @next/codemod@canary upgrade preview` was hanging forever when invoked by agents because the recommended-codemods multiselect blocks waiting on keyboard input. PR adds `-y, --yes` to `next-codemod upgrade` AND auto-detects `!process.stdin.isTTY` (so agents/CI get non-interactive mode without needing to know the flag). In non-interactive mode every prompt accepts its existing default: React 18-or-19 → upgrade (`shouldStayOnReact18 = false`); Turbopack enable → yes; custom-dev-script fallback → leave untouched (can't ask for replacement without TTY); recommended codemods multiselect → apply all (matches `selected: true` on every choice, logged to stdout); React 19 + React 19 Types codemods → yes. Plumbed through 4 `suggest*` helpers, each short-circuits before its `prompts(...)` call and returns the default. Full details in setup.md → `@next/codemod upgrade --yes` for agents/CI; **(H) [PR #94234](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94234) — `Turbopack: compute code hash per "use cache" function`** by mischnic, merged 2026-06-30T12:50:55Z — implementation hash per `use cache` function (includes the module + transitive imports; for bundled code hashes the generated code; for NFT'd files hashes the file content on disk). New `experimental.durableUseCacheEntries?: boolean` flag in `experimentalSchema` (`packages/next/src/server/config-schema.ts`) plus its `ExperimentalConfig` declaration (`config-shared.ts`). **Turbopack-only.** Surface shows up as a new `codeHash` field in `.next/server/server-reference-manifest.json`. Disabled in dev. Known limitation: `final_read_hint` makes some chunk items codegen twice. Multiple `use cache` functions in a single file share the same hash. Full details in performance.md → durable use cache code hash; **(I) [PR #95281](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95281) — `Fix workers on thread to not crash with napi-rs`** by jimmyhmiller, merged 2026-06-30T19:38:35Z — Turbopack worker-on-thread crashes traced to a [napi-rs bug](https://github.com/napi-rs/napi-rs/issues/3357) about dropping things off the isolate; workaround now in place so worker threads don't crash. Default still set to process workers (so impact is minimal until user opts into thread workers); **(J) [PR #95282](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95282) — `Upgrade React from 68631c04-20260626 to 92f4fda3-20260629`** by Vercel Release Bot, merged 2026-06-30T20:10:46Z — vendored React bump inside `packages/next/src/compiled/react/cjs/react.production.js`. no user-facing impact.. **Full canary.71 PR-by-PR breakdown in the existing canary.71 paragraph below this opening line**; **16.3.0-canary.65** released June 24, 2026 (Cache Components docs clarify `allow-runtime` / sync-IO / `instant = false` / CLS fallback ([#94997](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94997)), `cacheMaxMemorySize: 0` no longer forces a dynamic cache life in dev so a fully cached route can prerender again ([#95100](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95100)), `getHeaders` no longer mutates `req.headers` when stripping internal headers — fixes a dev instant-navigation regression where the React dev overlay stayed in "Rendering..." after a server action redirect because the wrong HTML request id was fabricated from a missing request id ([#95116](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95116))); **16.3.0-canary.71** released June 29, 2026 ([`dff50cc736`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/commit/dff50cc736) — 17 canary-branch commits since canary.70, total diff at [`compare/v16.3.0-canary.70...v16.3.0-canary.71`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/compare/v16.3.0-canary.70...v16.3.0-canary.71), tag cut at 2026-06-29T23:36:11Z, npm `dist-tag.next` pointer moved 2026-06-29T23:59:44Z). The 17 commits since canary.70 split into **eight material user-facing changes** (six Next.js canary-branch PRs + one Turbopack PR + one React canary PR that became the new canary-`92f4fda3-20260629`): **(1) fix: preserve repeated search params in client page segment cache keys** ([#94863](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/94863) by icyJoseph, merged June 29, 2026 at 11:54:29Z — silent stale-UI bug where `Object.fromEntries(new URLSearchParams(search))` collapsed `?foo=bar&foo=baz` to `{foo: ‘baz’}` in the client page segment cache key, causing cache collisions between `?color=red&color=blue` and `?color=blue`; affected `<Link>`, `router.replace`, `<form>` (next/form) soft-nav with repeated keys; fixed in `ppr-navigations.ts` + `segment-cache/scheduler.ts` + `route-params.ts` by switching to the existing `urlSearchParamsToParsedUrlQuery` helper which preserves repeated params as arrays; fixes issues [#92787](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/92787) (filed against next 16.2.1-canary.38, April 14), [#93104](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/93104) (filed against stable, April 21), and [#94821](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/94821) (filed against canary.51, June 15 — next/form submission case); new e2e test at `test/e2e/app-dir/multi-value-search-params-stale/`; no API change, no config change, no opt-in flag; single-value URLs unaffected; **upgrade to canary.71+ or 16.3.0 stable to get the fix**; full details in routing.md); **(2) fix: client nav URL bar stuck on original URL after middleware redirect + rewrite-to-dynamic-route** ([#95207](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/95207) by acdlite / Andrew Clark, merged June 29, 2026 at 17:00:28Z — silent stale-URL bug where a click on `<Link href="/a">` with a proxy/middleware redirect `/a → /` AND rewrite `/ → /a` (where `/a` is a dynamic route reading `await params`) rendered the correct page content but left `window.location.href` stuck on `/a` instead of `/`; only fires on soft-nav, hard-nav was always correct via the server resolver; root cause was the optimistic-URL-commit in the Instant Navigations reducer — the prediction was committed up front and the resolved RSC payload (after the redirect) was treated as confirmation of the prediction when the redirect target had the same shape as the prediction; fix invalidates the prediction on a redirect discovered during navigation and re-resolves the route before the address bar is shown; touches `ppr-navigations.ts` + `reducers/server-patch-reducer.ts` + `router-reducer-types.ts`; new e2e test at `test/e2e/app-dir/redirect-rewrite-dynamic/`; **fixes [issue #95195](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/95195) by amannn against 16.3.0-preview.5** with a [public Playwright reproduction repo](https://github.com/amannn/nextjs-16-3-bug-repro-redirect); full details in routing.md); **(3) Turbopack `experimental.cssChunking: ‘graph’` config schema breaking change** ([#95088](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95088) by sokra / Tobias Koppers, merged June 29, 2026 at 15:53:24Z — `moduleFactorCost` option REMOVED, replaced by `weightDistribution`; new per-group cost formula `chunk_group_weight * (chunk_size + request_cost)` where `chunk_group_weight = group_total_size ^ (-weightDistribution)`; defaults retuned: `requestCost` `20000` → `100000` bytes, `weightDistribution` default `0.1`; old `{ type: ‘graph’, requestCost, moduleFactorCost }` config throws a schema error in canary.71+ — string form `‘graph’` is unchanged and uses the new defaults automatically; full details in performance.md). **Plus [React canary `19.3.0-canary-92f4fda3-20260629`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react) (cut 2026-06-29T17:11:25Z, replaces canary `68631c04-20260626`)** — 3 commits ahead of the previous canary, one of which is material. *(As of 2026-06-30T16:54:01Z, the React canary SHA has moved forward to `19.3.0-canary-e2731312-20260630` (replaces `92f4fda3-20260629`). The new canary is non-material for the skill — its 6 commits are devtools-facade work and a chrome-devtools-mcp integration; no new APIs or fixes relevant to documented patterns.)* **[React PR #36851](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36851) `[Float] Forward nonce option in ReactDOM.preloadModule()`** by udit / Udit Dewan, merged June 29, 2026 at 11:33:22Z — silent CSP violation where `ReactDOM.preloadModule(href, { nonce, as, ... })` accepted `nonce` in its public type but the implementation only forwarded `as`, `crossOrigin`, and `integrity` to the host dispatcher, dropping `nonce`; under strict `script-src ‘nonce-...’` CSP, the emitted `<link rel="modulepreload">` had no nonce and was blocked by the browser; `ReactDOM.preload()` and `ReactDOM.preinitModule()` already forwarded `nonce` correctly; 5 files, +37/-5 across `ReactDOMFloat.js`, `ReactDOMTypes.js`, `ReactFlightClientConfigDOM.js`, and the test suite; full details in security.md). The other 6 canary-branch commits since canary.70 are non-material (skill docs, CI, React upgrade bump, type improvement, internal Turbopack): `cc-adoption` skill tighten by Aurora Scharff ([#95225](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95225), docs-only), `unstable_rethrow` client-bundling fix by eps1lon ([#95196](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95196), internal bundling optimization, Closes #95189), Turbopack `server-reference-manifest` dedup by Tariqul ([#95242](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95242), internal bug fix), CC test command display fix by eps1lon ([#95267](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95267), CI), React canary bump in Next.js by Vercel Release Bot ([#95211](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95211), package.json only), `decodeReply` typing by mischnic ([#95263](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95263), type narrowing), `[changelog] Add 19.0.x, 19.1.x, 19.2.x patch releases` by eps1lon ([#36898](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36898), changelog only), `[test] coverage for multi-byte code units in stream APIs` by eps1lon ([#36900](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36900), test only); **16.3.0-canary.70** released June 28, 2026 (npm dist-tag pointer moved 2026-06-29T00:02:51Z) — only one new content commit ([#95232](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95232) by mischnic, `Turbopack: make hash_xxh3_hash128 return u128` — pure Rust internal type-narrowing refactor, zero user-facing impact, all files under `turbopack/crates/`); **16.3.0-canary.69** released June 26, 2026 ([`4b164d7`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.3.0-canary.69), 15 commits since canary.68) — **fix: preserve middleware rewrite query in Pages API routes** ([#94905](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/94905), fixes issue [#94647](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/94647), June 10 — middleware rewrites from `/foo/bar?key=value` to `/api/proxy/bar?added=1&extra=2` were silently dropping the rewrite-added `added`/`extra` query params AND the destination catch-all `slug` param from `req.query` in Pages API handlers; full details in routing.md), **hard-navigate to app routes shadowed by a pages dynamic route** ([#95185](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/95185), shipped in canary.69 — soft-nav misroute fix, full details in routing.md), dev overlay Copy-as-prompt body restructured into step-by-step checklist ([#95186](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95186)), docs: insights error pages restructure + link to preview docs ([#95193](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95193)), docs: reorder blocking-prerender-dynamic fixes to Stream/Cache/Block ([#95198](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95198)), `next-dev-loop` requires `agent-browser >= 0.31.1` ([#95209](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95209)), Turbopack Rust toolchain bump ([#95205](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95205)), docs: fix preview message typo ([#95050](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95050)), Turbopack internal: revert `final_read_hint` ([#95178](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95178)) — first canary cut after the preview.5 stabilisation, so #94905 + #95185 are the two user-facing changes; everything else is docs/dev-overlay/agent-skill/tooling/internal metrics)
- **React 19.2.8** stable (shipped 2026-07-21T15:49:09Z, [GitHub release](https://github.com/facebook/react/releases/tag/v19.2.8); 19.2.7 → 19.2.8 diff is PR #37087 `[FlightReply] Performance improvements when decoding` + PR #36753 housekeeping) + **React 19.2.x canary** [`81e442ea-20260721`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react?activeTab=canary) (ships Fragment-blur fix + nested ViewTransition) — React Compiler 1.0 stable, `use()` hook, `useOptimistic`, `useFormStatus`, `useActionState`, `useEffectEvent`, `cacheSignal`, `cache`, `<Activity>`
- **TypeScript 6.0.3** (strict by default, ES2026 target, import defer) + **TypeScript 7.0.1-rc** (June 18, 2026, RC release of Go-based compiler ships as the main `typescript` package — `npx tsc` is now Go, ~10× faster, Strada internal API; stable targeted within 2 months of RC)
- **Zod 4.4.3** (14x faster string parsing, strict/loose object modes, `z.file()`, `z.templateLiteral()`)
- **Tailwind CSS v4.3.2** + **shadcn/ui 4.13.0** (CSS-first config via `@theme` directive — no tailwind.config.js by default; **shadcn 4.13.0 (July 3, 2026)** flips the default headless lib to **Base UI** (Radix still supported as the explicit alternative; per [official changelog](https://ui.shadcn.com/docs/changelog/2026-07-base-ui-default) this is the result of wide community adoption); **shadcn/typeset (July 14, 2026)** — new stream-friendly typography system (single CSS file, three CSS variables per container, no package, no config layer, container-aware, pairs naturally with shadcn 4.12.0 Chat Components for streaming markdown in chat UIs); **v4.3.2 (June 29, 2026) pure bug-fix patch** — `auto-rows-*` / `auto-cols-*` accept bare numeric values (#20229), `@tailwindcss/cli --watch` no longer crashes on Windows when `@source` points to a missing directory (#20242), `@tailwindcss/vite` no longer crashes in Deno v2.8.x (#20245), `@tailwindcss/cli --watch` no longer re-emits an unchanged file when only a sibling changes (#20249); v4.3.1 patch added `--silent` CLI flag, `@apply` with CSS mixins, cleaner spacing output; **4.11.1 (June 26, 2026) patches `shadcn add` to preserve existing `package.json` dependency specifiers** — fixes [shadcn-ui/ui#10525](https://github.com/shadcn-ui/ui/issues/10525) where the CLI could swap specifier values between packages, leaving lockfile + manifest contradictory; also drops the `node-fetch` transitive dep in favor of native `fetch` ([#10905](https://github.com/shadcn-ui/ui/pull/10905)); **4.12.0 (June 26, 2026, same day as 4.11.1) ships the first phase of Chat Components** — `MessageScroller` / `Message` / `Bubble` / `Attachment` / `Marker` (each available in Base UI + Radix UI flavors), two new CSS utilities `scroll-fade` + `shimmer` (shipped via `shadcn/tailwind.css`, so any project initialized with `npx shadcn@latest init` gets them for free), and a brand-new npm package **`@shadcn/react@0.1.0`** — unstyled headless React primitives, first primitive is `@shadcn/react/message-scroller` with the same interaction logic the registry item wraps)
- **Modern CSS 2026** — `field-sizing: content` (Baseline 2026, Firefox 152 just shipped), `@starting-style` (Baseline 2024), CSS Anchor Positioning (cross-engine 2025–26), OKLCH color space, `color-scheme` + `light-dark()` function, scroll-driven animations
- **Vite 8.1.0** (for non-Next projects; Next uses Turbopack — 8.1 ships WASM ESM Integration, `server.hmr` → `server.ws` rename, `import.meta.glob` `caseSensitive`, chunk importmap, lightningcss support, extended `server.fs.deny` defaults; **≥ 8.1.0 required for the new `caseSensitive` glob option**)
- **Vite 8.1.2** (June 30, 2026 patch — pure bug-fix release that supersedes 8.1.1; **8.1.2 is the recommended Vite version** because it: (1) reverts the es-module-lexer 2.2.0 upgrade in 8.1.1 (PR #22827) which broke any prod output chunk >~4 MB with `WebAssembly.Memory.grow()` error ([guybedford/es-module-lexer#214](https://github.com/guybedford/es-module-lexer/issues/214), reported as Vite [#22826](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/issues/22826)); (2) reverts PR #22687 multi-null-byte id escape from 8.1.1 because it broke the **waku** and **vite-plugin-rsc** ecosystem-ci suites; (3) re-applies the 8.1.1 pnpm `.modules.yaml` workspace-root resolution (PR #22825, with a corrected implementation of the originally-intended #22757); and (4) retains all the 8.1.1-intended fixes (`resolve.tsconfigPaths` CSS/Sass regression fix #22775, Bundled Dev Mode `import.meta.hot.invalidate()` stack overflow #22797, Bundled Dev Mode HMR/lazy-compile asset serving #22745, `esbuild.jsxSideEffects` → `oxc.jsx.pure` inversion #22809, `indexHtmlMiddleware` malformed URI crash #22781, missing sourcemap field in plugins #22782, `ERR_CLOSED_SERVER` in optimize-deps scanner #22784). **Skip 8.1.1 entirely** — go 8.1.0 → 8.1.2. See setup.md → Vite 8.1.2 for the long-form patterns, the `tsconfig.json` `include` requirement for CSS/Sass files, and the 8.1.1/8.1.2/es-module-lexer migration matrix)
- **@biomejs/biome 2.5.2** (recommended linter/formatter — 10–100x faster than ESLint, v2 has breaking changes from v1; run `npx biome migrate --write` after every upgrade)
- **TanStack Query v5.101.2** (React Query v5 — gcTime replaces cacheTime, improved SSR hydration, `skipToken` for dependent queries; **5.101.2 (June 27, 2026)** patches `@tanstack/query-devtools` — `setupStyleSheet` now sets `window.__nonce__` when `styleNonce` is passed ([#10736](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10736), fixes a silent CSP violation where the devtools' `<style>` tag was injected without a nonce and tripped strict `style-src 'nonce-...'` policies in production), `PiPContext.closePipWindow` now resets `pip_open` in localStorage to stop auto-reopen ([#10813](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10813)), `setupStyleSheet` dedup scoped to shadow-DOM target ([#10815](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10815)), `last-updated` sort returns 0 on ties so the comparator is deterministic ([#10812](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10812)), Theme sub-trigger className typo `position` → `theme` ([#10811](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10811)))
- **Vitest 4.1.9** (Browser Mode stable, Visual Regression testing via `toMatchScreenshot`, Playwright Trace support; requires Vite ≥ 6 + Node.js ≥ 20; **≥ 4.1.8 required for the CDP RCE fix (GHSA-g8mr-85jm-7xhm, CVSS 9.8)**; use `api.allowWrite: false, api.allowExec: false` in CI; **Vitest 5.0 in beta** — three beta releases between May 19 and June 15, 2026 (beta.3, beta.4, beta.5) introduced hard requirement Node 22 + Vite 6.4, strict `toHaveTextContent` + new `toMatchTextContent`, Browser Mode `locators.exact: true` default, `expect.poll` fails on timeout, hoistable-methods-out-of-scope throw, removed entry points (`vitest/coverage` → `vitest/node`, `vitest/environments` / `vitest/snapshot` → `vitest/runtime`, `vitest/runners` / `vitest/suite` → `TestRunner` from `vitest`, `vitest/mocker` → `@vitest/mocker`), `bench` moved into `test()` fixture, `test.sequential` → `{ concurrent: false }`, no ancestor-directory config lookup, `@vitest/runner` inlined into `vitest`, `TestModule.id` 1-based, `coverage.thresholds.perFile` accepts an object, browser orchestrator requires `sessionId`)
- **@playwright/test 1.61.1** (E2E — **1.61.0 (June 15, 2026)** shipped WebAuthn passkeys virtual authenticator via `browserContext.credentials.create/install`, `page.localStorage` / `page.sessionStorage` (origin-aware, replaces `page.evaluate('localStorage…')` boilerplate), `apiResponse.securityDetails()` / `serverAddr()` network parity with browser responses, `testOptions.video` modes parity with trace (`'on-all-retries'`, `'retain-on-first-failure'`, `'retain-on-failure-and-retries'`), `expect.soft.poll(...)`, `fullConfig.argv`, `fullConfig.failOnFlakyTests`, `testInfo.errors` AggregateError sub-entries, `-G` shorthand for `--grep-invert`, HAR + trace now include WebSocket frames, Ubuntu 26.04 host support, browsers Chromium 149.0.7827.55 / Firefox 151.0 / WebKit 26.5; **1.61.1 (June 23, 2026)** fixed five 1.61 regressions — `expect.extend` matcher-name shadowing built-ins, UI-mode API request byte count mismatch, trace-viewer WebSocket timestamps scaled by 1/1000, sync ESM loader crash on Node 22.15, pnpm workspace symlink `.ts` subpath resolution)
- **React Hook Form v7.80.0** + **@hookform/resolvers v5.4.0** (7.80.0 ships per-field `disabled` on `useFieldArray` items; broad perf pass; fixes `[]` vs `{}` `deepEqual` regression from 7.79.0) (compatible with Zod v4; v8.0.0-beta available with `createForm` API)
- **Node.js 24 LTS** (Node.js 22 LTS also supported)

## Pro Tips

- Use `create-next-app --typescript --tailwind --eslint --app --src-dir --import-alias "@/*"` for a fully typed Next.js project
- Run `npx tsc --noEmit` to type-check without building
- Use `shadcn@latest add <component>` to add components — they live in your codebase, not node_modules
- Use `next.config.ts` for all Next.js configuration (not `.js`)
- Use `react-hook-form` + `zod` for all forms — never manage form state with useState
- Enable React Compiler in `next.config.ts` with `reactCompiler: true` — eliminates most `useMemo`/`useCallback` manually
- Use Biome (`npx biome check`) for linting — 10–100x faster than ESLint
- Run `npx biome migrate --write` after every Biome upgrade to migrate your config for breaking changes
- Use `use cache` for all server-side data fetching in Next.js 16 — it's the explicit opt-in caching model

Start with `setup.md` to initialize a project, then `components.md` for shadcn/ui patterns.

## Next.js 16 Migration Notes

Key breaking changes from Next.js 15 → 16:

| Change | Impact |
|---|---|
| **`use cache` directive** replaces `unstable_cache` | New explicit caching API — mark data functions with `use cache` + `cacheTag` |
| **Implicit caching removed** — everything is dynamic by default | Old `fetch` caching patterns deprecated; use `use cache` explicitly |
| **PPR (Partial Prerendering) stable** — `cacheComponents: true` | Static shell + streaming dynamic content; no longer experimental |
| **Router scroll optimization** enabled by default | Previously scroll was reset on navigation; now preserved by default |
| Flat config default in `@next/eslint-plugin-next` | ESLint config format change |
| Deprecated `.turbo` config object removed | Use `turbopack` key in `next.config.ts` instead |
| `publicRuntimeConfig` / `serverRuntimeConfig` removed | Use environment variables directly |
| **`next lint` removed** | Use Biome (`npx biome check`) or ESLint (`npx eslint .`) directly |
| **`middleware.ts` deprecated → `proxy.ts`** | `middleware` export renamed to `proxy`; must be `async`; `matcher` moved to `next.config.ts` or exported from `proxy.ts` |

**Upgrade:** `npx @next/codemod@canary upgrade latest` (Next.js 16.2+) — automated upgrade CLI that handles codemods, config migrations, and breaking changes in one command. **In canary.72+ (PR #95312, June 30, 2026) accepts `-y, --yes` for agents/CI** — auto-enabled when stdin is not a TTY so AI coding agents no longer hang on the recommended-codemods multiselect. Alternatively `npx next upgrade` (interactive, Next.js 16.1+) or `npm install next@latest`.

## 1.4.77 — 2026-07-21T18:03Z

**JULY 2026 SECURITY RELEASE — ALL 9 CVEs NOW DOCUMENTED.** The July 21, 2026 security release shipped at ~16:00 UTC (16.2.11 / 15.5.21). Full CVE table added to `security.md`: 4 High (DoS via Server Actions, Turbopack middleware bypass with single i18n locale, SSRF via rewrites, SSRF via Server Actions on custom servers) + 5 Medium (Image Optimization SVG DoS, unbounded Edge payload, Server Function endpoint disclosure, cache confusion ×2). The live status checkpoints (T-24h through T+6h) removed and replaced with final resolution. `api.md` gained "Server Action Security Hardening (canary.92)" section covering 7 PRs: bodySizeLimit Edge enforcement, correct origin for custom-server redirects, exotic rewrite param encoding, server reference ID validation (closes CVE-2026-64643), basePath preservation in redirects, and two fetch cache key fixes (effective-request keying + byte-exact binary body hashing). `deployment.md` gained "July 2026 Security Releases" section documenting 16.2.11 (Active LTS) + 15.5.21 (Maintenance LTS) + canary.92 upgrade path, post-upgrade checklist, and the monthly cadence reminder (next: August 20, 2026). canary.92 non-security items: Turbopack middleware i18n single-locale fix, MPA form validation perf, SVG detectContentType perf, instant validation navigation fix, `use cache` error handling, docs AI agents guide expansion. React 19.2.8 (2026-07-21) — performance improvements when decoding RSC (PR #37087, eps1lon). TanStack Query 5.101.4 (2026-07-21) — patch on query-core 5.101.4.

Sources:
